An executable named `ledger` at the\napplication root dispatches `./ledger <command>` to Python command modules. Each command resolves the\ndatabase, validates every input, checks every rule against stored data, and then performs all of its\nwrites in one database transaction or none. Reports read stored data only.\n\n## Technology Stack\n\nDerived from `TECHNOLOGY_STACK.md`; that file is the decision of record.\n\n| Technology | Where it applies |\n|------------|------------------|\n| Python 3.11 or newer | All application code, tests and acceptance criteria. |\n| SQLite | The ledger store, reached only through the generated `persistence` package. |\n| CLI | The `ledger` executable and the `cli` package. |\n| pytest | The project test suite behind `bin/test.sh`. |\n| POSIX sh | `bin/test.sh` and the `ledger` launcher. |\n\n## Module Layout\n\n```text\nledger                  executable launcher (POSIX sh) at the application root\ncli/                    entry package: main(), errors, commands/, testing\n  __init__.py           main(argv) -> int\n  errors.py             CliError and its four subclasses\n  testing.py            cli_runner(*args) -> (exit_code, stdout, stderr)\n  commands/             one module per command, each exposing run(args) -> list[str]\nledgerlib/              domain code shared by commands\n  config.py             database location resolution and open_ledger()\n  csvout.py             cents formatting and CSV row helpers\n  validation.py         codes, names, memos, amounts, dates, ids\n  rules.py              balances, non-negative rule, transaction id allocation\npersistence/            generated by Drydock from DATABASE.md; never edited by hand\ntests/                  project pytest suite\nbin/test.sh             application test runner\npyproject.toml, uv.lock project manifest and committed lockfile\n```\n\nThe domain package is named `ledgerlib` because the executable `ledger` occupies that name at the\napplication root.\n\n## Module Ownership\n\n| Boundary | Owning module | Low-level APIs it may access |\n|----------|---------------|------------------------------|\n| Persistence | `persistence` (generated) | The `sqlite3` driver. No other module imports a database driver. |\n| Configuration | `ledgerlib.config` | `os.environ`, filesystem path creation for the database file's parent. |\n| Output formatting | `ledgerlib.csvout` | Integer arithmetic and string joining only. |\n| Input validation | `ledgerlib.validation` | `re`, `datetime.date` for calendar checks. |\n| Business rules | `ledgerlib.rules` | `db.<table>.<operation>(...)` only. |\n| Process boundary | `cli` | `sys.stdout`, `sys.stderr`, exit codes. |\n| File stores | none | Load commands read the CSV path the user names; no other file store. |\n| External services | none | No network access. |\n\n## Technical Decisions\n\n- Money is integer cents at every layer. Floating point is never used for money. Output formatting\n  and parsing use integer arithmetic.\n- Commands return output lines; `cli.main` writes them to standard output only after the command\n  succeeds, so a failing command writes nothing to standard output and one diagnostic line to\n  standard error.\n- Exit codes: 0 success, 1 operational failure, 2 usage error, 3 rejected, 4 not found. They are\n  carried by `CliError` subclasses.\n- Every command checks all input and all balance rules before its first write. Reads that feed a\n  write, such as the next transaction id, occur in the same database transaction as the write.\n- Reports and balances are computed in Python from list rows; no aggregate is stored.\n- Application code never reads `sources/run.sh` or any seed file, and never embeds a report answer.\n- Only SQLite is supported; a PostgreSQL DSN is refused with exit 1 and nothing is written.\n- There is no authentication; access is governed by file permissions on the database.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC foundation-python-version ===\nIntent: The project runs on Python 3.11 or newer as the technology stack requires.\n\nimport sys\n\nminimum = (3, 11)\nassert sys.version_info[:2] >= minimum\n=== END AC foundation-python-version ===\n\n=== AC foundation-pyproject ===\nIntent: The project manifest parses as TOML and declares a project with a Python requirement.\n\nimport tomllib\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nmanifest = Path(\"pyproject.toml\")\nassert manifest.is_file()\nwith manifest.open(\"rb\") as handle:\n    data = tomllib.load(handle)\nproject = data[\"project\"]\nassert \"requires-python\" in project\nassert isinstance(project[\"requires-python\"], str)\n=== END AC foundation-pyproject ===\n\n=== AC foundation-lockfile ===\nIntent: A committed uv lockfile sits beside the manifest so dependencies are reproducible.\n\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nlock = Path(\"uv.lock\")\nassert lock.is_file()\nassert lock.stat().st_size > 0\n=== END AC foundation-lockfile ===\n\n=== AC foundation-test-tree ===\nIntent: The pytest tree has a conftest and a tests directory so the runner can collect the suite.\n\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nassert Path(\"tests\").is_dir()\ncandidates = [Path(\"conftest.py\"), Path(\"tests/conftest.py\")]\nassert any(p.is_file() for p in candidates)\n=== END AC foundation-test-tree ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- Do not use floating point for money at any layer.\n- Do not open a database driver outside the generated `persistence` package.\n- Do not read `sources/run.sh` or any seed file from application code.\n- Do not hardcode a report row, count or total.\n- Do not modify anything under `sources/`.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-CliEntry.md ===\n# FEATURE: CLI Entry\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261027 V1 |\n| Description | The `ledger` executable, the `cli` dispatcher and the exit-code error mapping. |\n| Depends On | ARCHITECTURE.md, DATABASE.md |\n| Provides | ledger, cli.main |\n| Consumes | python-project, persistence |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nGive every command one entry point with one failure contract: output is buffered and written only on\nsuccess, and any failure produces one line on standard error, nothing on standard output, and a\nconventional exit code.\n\n## Interface\n\n- `ledger` at the application root is an executable POSIX sh launcher. It runs\n  `cli.main(sys.argv[1:])` with `.venv/bin/python` when present, otherwise `python3`, and exits with\n  the returned value.\n- `cli.main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int`.\n- `cli/testing.py` provides `cli_runner(*args) -> (exit_code, stdout, stderr)`.\n- `cli/errors.py` defines `CliError` with an `exit_code` attribute and the subclasses\n  `OperationalError` (1), `UsageError` (2), `RejectedError` (3) and `NotFoundError` (4).\n- `cli/commands/` is a package. `argv[0]` names the module `cli.commands.<command>`, imported\n  lazily; each module exposes `run(args: list[str]) -> list[str]` returning output lines and\n  printing nothing.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- With no arguments, or when the command module does not exist, `main` raises a usage error:\n  exit 2, nothing on standard output, one diagnostic line on standard error.\n- On success `main` writes the returned lines to standard output, each ended by `\\n`, and returns 0.\n- A `CliError` raised by a command produces one diagnostic line on standard error, nothing on\n  standard output, and the error's `exit_code`.\n- Any other exception is an operational failure: exit 1, one diagnostic line, nothing on standard\n  output.\n- The remaining arguments after the command name are passed to `run` unchanged.\n- No command logic lives in this feature.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- None — persistence is declared by DATABASE.md and reached through ledgerlib by later features.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC cli-no-arguments ===\nIntent: Calling main with no arguments is a usage error with exit 2 and an empty standard output.\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\n\ncode, out, err = cli_runner()\nprint(out)\nprint(err)\nassert code == 2\nassert out == \"\"\n=== END AC cli-no-arguments ===\n\n=== AC cli-unknown-command ===\nIntent: An unknown command exits 2, writes nothing to standard output and one diagnostic line to standard error.\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\n\ncode, out, err = cli_runner(\"zzunknowncommand\")\nprint(out)\nprint(err)\nassert code == 2\nassert out == \"\"\nassert len(err.strip().splitlines()) == 1\n=== END AC cli-unknown-command NotFoundError, OperationalError, RejectedError, UsageError\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\n\nexpected = {OperationalError: 1, UsageError: 2, RejectedError: 3, NotFoundError: 4}\nfor error_class, status in expected.items():\n    def run(args, error_class=error_class):\n        raise error_class(\"probe failure\")\n\n    module = types.ModuleType(\"cli.commands.zzprobe\")\n    module.run = run\n    sys.modules[\"cli.commands.zzprobe\"] = module\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(\"zzprobe\")\n    print(code, repr(out), repr(err))\n    assert code == status\n    assert error_class(\"x\").exit_code == status\n    assert out == \"\"\n    assert len(err.strip().splitlines()) == 1\nsys.modules.pop(\"cli.commands.zzprobe\", None)\n=== END AC cli-error-mapping ===\n\n=== received.append(list(args))\n    return lines\n\nmodule = types.ModuleType(\"cli.commands.zzok\")\nmodule.run = run\nsys.modules[\"cli.commands.zzok\"] = module\ncode, out, err = cli_runner(\"zzok\", |\n| Description | The POSIX `bin/test.sh` runner for the application's own pytest suite. |\n| Depends On | ARCHITECTURE.md |\n| Provides | bin/test.sh |\n| Consumes | python-project |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nGive the application one command that runs its automated tests from the application root and exits\nzero only when every test passes. This is the verification command for every behavioral story.\n\n## Interface\n\n`sh bin/test.sh`:\n\n- changes to the application root regardless of the caller's directory;\n- activates `.venv` when it exists;\n- runs `pytest -q`;\n- exits with pytest's exit status, so it is zero only when every test passes.\n\nThe script uses POSIX sh only. No test may itself invoke `bin/test.sh`. At least one meaningful\nproject test exists under `tests/` so the runner has something to run.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- None — this feature reaches no repository.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC runner-script-exists ===\nIntent: The runner script exists at bin/test.sh and invokes pytest.\n\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nscript = Path(\"bin/test.sh\")\nassert script.is_file()\ntext = script.read_text()\nassert \"pytest\" in text\n=== END AC runner-script-exists ===\n\n=== AC runner-posix-syntax ===\nIntent: The runner parses under a POSIX shell without syntax errors.\n\nimport subprocess\n\nresult = subprocess.run([\"sh\", \"-n\", \"bin/test.sh\"], capture_output=True, text=True)\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.returncode == 0\n=== END AC runner-posix-syntax ===\n\n=== AC runner-has-tests ===\nIntent: The tests directory contains at least one test module for the runner to collect.\n\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nmodules = sorted(Path(\"tests\").glob(\"test_*.py\"))\nprint([str(m) for m in modules])\nassert len(modules) >= 1\n=== END AC runner-has-tests ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- The script is POSIX sh, not bash.\n- No test invokes `bin/test.sh`.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-DatabaseSelection.md ===\n# FEATURE: Database Selection\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261027 V1 |\n| Description | Resolve the ledger database location from configuration and open it through the persistence library. |\n| Depends On | FEATURE-CliEntry.md, DATABASE.md |\n| Provides | ledgerlib.config |\n| Consumes | cli.main, persistence |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nDecide which database a command uses and refuse unsupported engines before anything is written.\n\n## Interface\n\n`ledgerlib/config.py`:\n\n- `resolve_database(environ: Mapping[str, str] | None = None) -> str` returns the SQLite location\n  using `DATABASE_URL`, else `DATABASE_PATH`, else `data/app.db`. A leading `sqlite://` is removed.\n  `environ` defaults to `os.environ`; an unset or empty value counts as absent.\n- A value beginning `postgres://` or `postgresql://` raises `OperationalError` (exit 1) before any\n  file or directory is created or written.\n- `open_ledger(environ: Mapping[str, str] | None = None)` returns an open persistence `Database` for\n  the resolved location via `open_database`. An unreachable location, or an existing file that is\n  not a valid database, raises `OperationalError`. Nothing is written to standard output.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- None — persistence is declared by DATABASE.md and reached through `open_database`.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC config-url-wins ===\nIntent: DATABASE_URL takes precedence over DATABASE_PATH.\n\nfrom ledgerlib.config import resolve_database\n\nurl_value = \"zz_url_choice.db\"\npath_value = \"zz_path_choice.db\"\nenviron = {\"DATABASE_URL\": url_value, \"DATABASE_PATH\": path_value}\nassert resolve_database(environ) == url_value\n=== END AC config-url-wins ===\n\n=== AC config-path-fallback ===\nIntent: DATABASE_PATH is used when DATABASE_URL is unset.\n\nfrom ledgerlib.config import resolve_database\n\npath_value = \"zz_path_only.db\"\nassert resolve_database({\"DATABASE_PATH\": path_value}) == path_value\n=== END AC config-path-fallback ===\n\n=== AC config-default ===\nIntent: With neither variable set the default location data/app.db is BEGIN ARTIFACT ARCHITECTURE.md ===\n# ARCHITECTURE: LayersTesting Cash Ledger\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261027 V1 |\n| Description | Modules, boundaries and technical decisions for the `ledger` command-line cash ledger. |\n| Provides | python-project |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Overview\n\n`ledger` is a single-process command-line application. An executable named `ledger` at the\napplication root dispatches `./ledger <command>` to Python command modules. Each command resolves the\ndatabase, validates every input, checks every rule against stored data, and then performs all of its\nwrites in one database transaction or none. Reports read stored data only.\n\n## Technology Stack\n\nDerived from `TECHNOLOGY_STACK.md`; that file is the decision of record.\n\n| Technology | Where it applies |\n|------------|------------------|\n| Python 3.11 or newer | All application code, tests and acceptance criteria. |\n| SQLite | The ledger store, reached only through the generated `persistence` package. |\n| CLI | The `ledger` executable and the `cli` package. |\n| pytest | The project test suite behind `bin/test.sh`. |\n| POSIX sh | `bin/test.sh` and the `ledger` launcher. |\n\n## Module Layout\n\n```text\nledger                  executable launcher (POSIX sh) at the application root\ncli/                    entry package: main(), errors, commands/, testing\n  __init__.py           main(argv) -> int\n  errors.py             CliError and its four subclasses\n  testing.py            cli_runner(*args) -> (exit_code, stdout, stderr)\n  commands/             one module per command, each exposing run(args) -> list[str]\nledgerlib/              domain code shared by commands\n  config.py             database location resolution and open_ledger()\n  csvout.py             cents formatting and CSV row helpers\n  validation.py         codes, names, memos, amounts, dates, ids\n  rules.py              balances, non-negative rule, transaction id allocation\npersistence/            generated by Drydock from DATABASE.md; never edited by hand\ntests/                  project pytest suite\nbin/test.sh             application test runner\npyproject.toml, uv.lock project manifest and committed lockfile\n```\n\nThe domain package is named `ledgerlib` because the executable `ledger` occupies that name at the\napplication root.\n\n## Module Ownership\n\n| Boundary | Owning module | Low-level APIs it may access |\n|----------|---------------|------------------------------|\n| Persistence | `persistence` (generated) | The `sqlite3` driver. No other module imports a database driver. |\n| Configuration | `ledgerlib.config` | `os.environ`, filesystem path creation for the database file's parent. |\n| Output formatting | `ledgerlib.csvout` | Integer arithmetic and string joining only. |\n| Input validation | `ledgerlib.validation` | `re`, `datetime.date` for calendar checks. |\n| Business rules | `ledgerlib.rules` | `db.<table>.<operation>(...)` only. |\n| Process boundary | `cli` | `sys.stdout`, `sys.stderr`, exit codes. |\n| File stores | none | Load commands read the CSV path the user names; no other file store. |\n| External services | none | No network access. |\n\n## Technical Decisions\n\n- Money is integer cents at every layer. Floating point is never used for money. Output formatting\n  and parsing use integer arithmetic.\n- Commands return output lines; `cli.main` writes them to standard output only after the command\n  succeeds, so a failing command writes nothing to standard output and one diagnostic line to\n  standard error.\n- Exit codes: 0 success, 1 operational failure, 2 usage error, 3 rejected, 4 not found. They are\n  carried by `CliError` subclasses.\n- Every command checks all input and all balance rules before its first write. Reads that feed a\n  write, such as the next transaction id, occur in the same database transaction as the write.\n- Reports and balances are computed in Python from list rows; no aggregate is stored.\n- Application code never reads `sources/run.sh` or any seed file, and never embeds a report answer.\n- Only SQLite is supported; a PostgreSQL DSN is refused with exit 1 and nothing is written.\n- There is no authentication; access is governed by file permissions on the database.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC foundation-python-version ===\nIntent: The project runs on Python 3.11 or newer as the technology stack requires.\n\nimport sys\n\nminimum = (3, 11)\nassert sys.version_info[:2] >= minimum\n=== END AC foundation-python-version ===\n\n=== AC foundation-pyproject ===\nIntent: The project manifest parses as TOML and declares a project with a Python requirement.\n\nimport tomllib\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nmanifest = Path(\"pyproject.toml\")\nassert manifest.is_file()\nwith manifest.open(\"rb\") as handle:\n    data = tomllib.load(handle)\nproject = data[\"project\"]\nassert \"requires-python\" in project\nassert isinstance(project[\"requires-python\"], str)\n=== END AC foundation-pyproject ===\n\n=== AC foundation-lockfile ===\nIntent: A committed uv lockfile sits beside the manifest so dependencies are reproducible.\n\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nlock = Path(\"uv.lock\")\nassert lock.is_file()\nassert lock.stat().st_size > 0\n=== END AC foundation-lockfile ===\n\n=== AC foundation-test-tree ===\nIntent: The pytest tree has a conftest and a tests directory so the runner can collect the suite.\n\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nassert Path(\"tests\").is_dir()\ncandidates = [Path(\"conftest.py\"), Path(\"tests/conftest.py\")]\nassert any(p.is_file() for p in candidates)\n=== END AC foundation-test-tree ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- Do not use floating point for money at any layer.\n- Do not open a database driver outside the generated `persistence` package.\n- Do not read `sources/run.sh` or any seed file from application code.\n- Do not hardcode a report row, count or total.\n- Do not modify anything under `sources/`.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-CliEntry.md ===\n# FEATURE: CLI Entry\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261027 V1 |\n| Description | The `ledger` executable, the `cli` dispatcher and the exit-code error mapping. |\n| Depends On | ARCHITECTURE.md, DATABASE.md |\n| Provides | ledger, cli.main |\n| Consumes | python-project, persistence |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nGive every command one entry point with one failure contract: output is buffered and written only on\nsuccess, and any failure produces one line on standard error, nothing on standard output, and a\nconventional exit code.\n\n## Interface\n\n- `ledger` at the application root is an executable POSIX sh launcher. It runs\n  `cli.main(sys.argv[1:])` with `.venv/bin/python` when present, otherwise `python3`, and exits with\n  the returned value.\n- `cli.main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int`.\n- `cli/testing.py` provides `cli_runner(*args) -> (exit_code, stdout, stderr)`.\n- `cli/errors.py` defines `CliError` with an `exit_code` attribute and the subclasses\n  `OperationalError` (1), `UsageError` (2), `RejectedError` (3) and `NotFoundError` (4).\n- `cli/commands/` is a package. `argv[0]` names the module `cli.commands.<command>`, imported\n  lazily; each module exposes `run(args: list[str]) -> list[str]` returning output lines and\n  printing nothing.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- With no arguments, or when the command module does not exist, `main` raises a usage error:\n  exit 2, nothing on standard output, one diagnostic line on standard error.\n- On success `main` writes the returned lines to standard output, each ended by `\\n`, and returns 0.\n- A `CliError` raised by a command produces one diagnostic line on standard error, nothing on\n  standard output, and the error's `exit_code`.\n- Any other exception is an operational failure: exit 1, one diagnostic line, nothing on standard\n  output.\n- The remaining arguments after the command name are passed to `run` unchanged.\n- No command logic lives in this feature.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- None — persistence is declared by DATABASE.md and reached through ledgerlib by later features.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC cli-no-arguments ===\nIntent: Calling main with no arguments is a usage error with exit 2 and an empty standard output.\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\n\ncode, out, err = cli_runner()\nprint(out)\nprint(err)\nassert code == 2\nassert out == \"\"\n=== END AC cli-no-arguments ===\n\n=== AC cli-unknown-command ===\nIntent: An unknown command exits 2, writes nothing to standard output and one diagnostic line to standard error.\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\n\ncode, out, err = cli_runner(\"zzunknowncommand\")\nprint(out)\nprint(err)\nassert code == 2\nassert out == \"\"\nassert len(err.strip().splitlines()) == 1\n=== END AC cli-unknown-command ===\n\n=== AC cli-error-mapping ===\nIntent: Each CliError subclass maps to its exit code, with empty standard output and one standard error line.\n\nimport sys\nimport types\n\nfrom cli.errors import NotFoundError, OperationalError, RejectedError, UsageError\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\n\nexpected = {OperationalError: 1, UsageError: 2, RejectedError: 3, NotFoundError: 4}\nfor error_class, status in expected.items():\n    def run(args, error_class=error_class):\n        raise error_class(\"probe failure\")\n\n    module = types.ModuleType(\"cli.commands.zzprobe\")\n    module.run = run\n    sys.modules[\"cli.commands.zzprobe\"] = module\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(\"zzprobe\")\n    print(code, repr(out), repr(err))\n    assert code == status\n    assert error_class(\"x\").exit_code == status\n    assert out == \"\"\n    assert len(err.strip().splitlines()) == 1\nsys.modules.pop(\"cli.commands.zzprobe\", None)\n=== END AC cli-error-mapping ===\n\n=== AC cli-unexpected-exception ===\nIntent: An unexpected exception from a command is an operational failure with exit 1 and empty standard output.\n\nimport sys\nimport types\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\n\ndef run(args):\n    raise RuntimeError(\"probe crash\")\n\nmodule = types.ModuleType(\"cli.commands.zzcrash\")\nmodule.run = run\nsys.modules[\"cli.commands.zzcrash\"] = module\ncode, out, err = cli_runner(\"zzcrash\")\nsys.modules.pop(\"cli.commands.zzcrash\", None)\nprint(code, repr(out), repr(err))\nassert code == 1\nassert out == \"\"\nassert len(err.strip().splitlines()) == 1\n=== END AC cli-unexpected-exception ===\n\n=== AC cli-success-output ===\nIntent: A successful command's lines reach standard output one per line with newline endings, and arguments pass through received.append(list(args))\n    return lines\n\nmodule = types.ModuleType(\"cli.commands.zzok\")\nmodule.run = run\nsys.modules[\"cli.commands.zzok\"] = module\ncode, out, err = cli_runner(\"zzok\", \"zzunknowncommand\"], capture_output=True, text=True)\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.returncode == 2\nassert result.stdout == \"\"\n=== END AC cli-executable-launcher ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- A failing command writes nothing to standard output.\n- Diagnostics go to standard error and are never compared.\n- This feature contains no command logic and opens no database driver.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-TestRunner.md ===\n# FEATURE: Test Runner\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261027 V1 |\n| Description | The POSIX `bin/test.sh` runner for the application's own pytest suite. |\n| Depends On | ARCHITECTURE.md |\n| Provides | bin/test.sh |\n| Consumes | python-project |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nGive the application one command that runs its automated tests from the application root and exits\nzero only when every test passes. This is the verification command for every behavioral story.\n\n## Interface\n\n`sh bin/test.sh`:\n\n- changes to the application root regardless of the caller's directory;\n- activates `.venv` when it exists;\n- runs `pytest -q`;\n- exits with pytest's exit status, so it is zero only when every test passes.\n\nThe script uses POSIX sh only. No test may itself invoke `bin/test.sh`. At least one meaningful\nproject test exists under `tests/` so the runner has something to run.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- None — this feature reaches no repository.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC runner-script-exists ===\nIntent: The runner script exists at bin/test.sh and invokes pytest.\n\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nscript = Path(\"bin/test.sh\")\nassert script.is_file()\ntext = script.read_text()\nassert \"pytest\" in text\n=== END AC runner-script-exists ===\n\n=== AC runner-posix-syntax ===\nIntent: The runner parses under a POSIX shell without syntax errors.\n\nimport subprocess\n\nresult = subprocess.run([\"sh\", \"-n\", \"bin/test.sh\"], capture_output=True, text=True)\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.returncode == 0\n=== END AC runner-posix-syntax ===\n\n=== AC runner-has-tests ===\nIntent: The tests directory contains at least one test module for the runner to collect.\n\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nmodules = sorted(Path(\"tests\").glob(\"test_*.py\"))\nprint([str(m) for m in modules])\nassert len(modules) >= 1\n=== END AC runner-has-tests ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- The script is POSIX sh, not bash.\n- No test invokes `bin/test.sh`.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-DatabaseSelection.md ===\n# FEATURE: Database Selection\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261027 V1 |\n| Description | Resolve the ledger database location from configuration and open it through the persistence library. |\n| Depends On | FEATURE-CliEntry.md, DATABASE.md |\n| Provides | ledgerlib.config |\n| Consumes | cli.main, persistence |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nDecide which database a command uses and refuse unsupported engines before anything is written.\n\n## Interface\n\n`ledgerlib/config.py`:\n\n- `resolve_database(environ: Mapping[str, str] | None = None) -> str` returns the SQLite location\n  using `DATABASE_URL`, else `DATABASE_PATH`, else `data/app.db`. A leading `sqlite://` is removed.\n  `environ` defaults to `os.environ`; an unset or empty value counts as absent.\n- A value beginning `postgres://` or `postgresql://` raises `OperationalError` (exit 1) before any\n  file or directory is created or written.\n- `open_ledger(environ: Mapping[str, str] | None = None)` returns an open persistence `Database` for\n  the resolved location via `open_database`. An unreachable location, or an existing file that is\n  not a valid database, raises `OperationalError`. Nothing is written to standard output.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- None — persistence is declared by DATABASE.md and reached through `open_database`.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC config-url-wins ===\nIntent: DATABASE_URL takes precedence over DATABASE_PATH.\n\nfrom ledgerlib.config import resolve_database\n\nurl_value = \"zz_url_choice.db\"\npath_value = \"zz_path_choice.db\"\nenviron = {\"DATABASE_URL\": url_value, \"DATABASE_PATH\": path_value}\nassert resolve_database(environ) == url_value\n=== END AC config-url-wins ===\n\n=== AC config-path-fallback ===\nIntent: DATABASE_PATH is used when DATABASE_URL is unset.\n\nfrom ledgerlib.config import resolve_database\n\npath_value = \"zz_path_only.db\"\nassert resolve_database({\"DATABASE_PATH\": path_value}) == path_value\n=== END AC config-path-fallback ===\n\n=== AC config-default ===\nIntent: With neither variable set the default location data/app.db is used.\n\nfrom ledgerlib.config import resolve_database\n\nassert resolve_database({}) == \"data/app.db\"\n=== END AC config-default ===\n\n=== AC config-sqlite-prefix ===\nIntent: An optional sqlite:// prefix is stripped from the location.\n\nfrom ledgerlib.config import resolve_database\n\nlocation = \"zz_prefixed.db\"\nassert resolve_database({\"DATABASE_URL\": \"sqlite://\" + location}) == location\n=== END AC config-sqlite-prefix ===\n\n=== AC config-postgres-refused ===\nIntent: A PostgreSQL DSN is refused with exit code 1 and no database file is created.\n\nimport os\nimport tempfile\n\nfrom cli.errors import OperationalError\nfrom ledgerlib.config import resolve_database\n\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:\n    would_create = os.path.join(tmp, \"nested\", \"ledger.db\")\n    for dsn in (\"postgres://localhost/ledger\", \"postgresql://localhost/ledger\"):\n        environ = {\"DATABASE_URL\": dsn, \"DATABASE_PATH\": would_create}\n        raised = None\n        try:\n            resolve_database(environ)\n        except OperationalError as exc:\n            raised = exc\n        assert raised is not None\n        assert raised.exit_code == 1\n    assert not os.path.exists(would_create)\n    assert not os.path.exists(os.path.dirname(would_create))\n=== END AC config-postgres-refused ===\n\n=== AC config-open-ledger ===\nIntent: open_ledger returns a database exposing the declared tables that can be read.\n\nfrom ledgerlib.config import open_ledger\n\ndb = open_ledger()\nrows = db.accounts.list()\nassert isinstance(rows, list)\n=== END AC config-open-ledger ===\n\n=== AC config-invalid-database-file ===\nIntent: An existing file that is not a valid database makes open_ledger fail with exit code 1 and print nothing.\n\nimport contextlib\nimport io\nimport os\nimport tempfile\n\nfrom cli.errors import OperationalError\nfrom ledgerlib.config import open_ledger\n\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:\n    bogus = os.path.join(tmp, \"not_a_database.db\")\n    with open(bogus, \"wb\") as handle:\n        handle.write(b\"x\" * 4096)\n    out = io.StringIO()\n    raised = None\n    with contextlib.redirect_stdout(out):\n        try:\n            db = open_ledger({\"DATABASE_URL\": bogus})\n            db.accounts.list()\n        except OperationalError as exc:\n            raised = exc\n    assert raised is not None\n    assert raised.exit_code == 1\n    assert out.getvalue() == \"\"\n=== END AC config-invalid-database-file ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- No database driver is imported outside the `persistence` package.\n- A PostgreSQL DSN never causes a file or directory to be created.\n- Nothing is written to standard output on failure.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-CsvOutput.md ===\n# FEATURE: CSV Output\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261027 V1 |\n| Description | Format integer cents and CSV rows exactly as the output contract requires. |\n| Depends On | FEATURE-CliEntry.md |\n| Provides | ledgerlib.csvout |\n| Consumes | cli.main |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nOne place formats money and rows, so every command prints amounts with exactly two decimal places and\nrows with no quoting, no trailing spaces and no thousands separators.\n\n## Interface\n\n`ledgerlib/csvout.py`:\n\n- `format_cents(cents: int) -> str` writes integer cents with exactly two decimals, a leading `-`\n  when negative, and no thousands separator. It uses integer arithmetic only, so zero is `0.00`\n  and never `-0.00`. A non-integer (including `float` and `bool`) raises `TypeError`.\n- `csv_line(fields) -> str` joins fields with commas, with no quoting and no trailing newline;\n  `cli.main` adds the `\\n` ending.\n- `ACCOUNT_HEADER` is `account,name,transactions,balance`.\n- `TRANSACTION_HEADER` is `txn_id,date,account,category,amount,memo`.\n- `account_row(account, name, transactions, balance_cents) -> str`.\n- `transaction_row(txn_id, date, account, category, amount_cents, memo) -> str`.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- None — this feature formats values and reaches no repository.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC csv-format-examples ===\nIntent: Cents format with two decimals, a leading minus, no separators, and zero is never negative.\n\nfrom ledgerlib.csvout import format_cents\n\nassert format_cents(-1250) == \"-12.50\"\nassert format_cents(0) == \"0.00\"\nassert format_cents(500000) == \"5000.00\"\nassert format_cents(5) == \"0.05\"\nassert format_cents(-5) == \"-0.05\"\nassert format_cents(123456789) == \"1234567.89\"\n=== END AC csv-format-examples ===\n\n=== AC csv-format-roundtrip ===\nIntent: For many values the text has the two-decimal shape and its decimal value equals cents divided by one hundred.\n\nimport re\nfrom decimal import Decimal\n\nfrom ledgerlib.csvout import format_cents\n\nshape = re.compile(r\"-?\\d+\\.\\d\\d\")\nfor cents in range(-250000, 250001, 997):\n    text = format_cents(cents)\n    assert shape.fullmatch(text), text\n    assert \",\" not in text\n    assert Decimal(text) == Decimal(cents) / Decimal(100)\n    assert not text.startswith(\"-0.00\")\n=== END AC csv-format-roundtrip ===\n\n=== AC csv-format-rejects-float ===\nIntent: Money is never formatted from floating point; a float or bool input raises TypeError.\n\nfrom ledgerlib.csvout import format_cents\n\nfor bad in (1.5, 100.0, True):\n    raised = False\n    try:\n        format_cents(bad)\n    except TypeError:\n        raised = True\n    assert raised\n=== END AC csv-format-rejects-float ===\n\n=== AC csv-line-join ===\nIntent: Rows join with commas, with no quoting and no trailing space or newline.\n\nfrom ledgerlib.csvout import csv_line\n\nfields = [\"alpha\", \"beta\", \"gamma\"]\nline = csv_line(fields)\nassert line == \",\".join(fields)\nassert '\"' not in line\nassert line == line.rstrip()\nassert \"\\n\" not in line\n=== END AC csv-line-join ===\n\n=== AC csv-headers ===\nIntent: The shared header constants match the output contract's account and transaction columns.\n\nfrom ledgerlib.csvout import ACCOUNT_HEADER, TRANSACTION_HEADER\n\nassert ACCOUNT_HEADER == \"account,name,transactions,balance\"\nassert TRANSACTION_HEADER == \"txn_id,date,account,category,amount,memo\"\n=== END AC csv-headers ===\n\n=== AC csv-account-row ===\nIntent: The account row carries code, name, transaction count and a two-decimal balance in header order.\n\nfrom decimal import Decimal\n\nfrom ledgerlib.csvout import ACCOUNT_HEADER, account_row\n\ncode = \"ZZACCT\"\nname = \"Zz Account\"\ncount = 3\nbalance = 12345\nfields = account_row(code, name, count, balance).split(\",\")\nassert len(fields) == \"2026-04-05\"\naccount = \"ZZACCT\"\ncategory = \"ZZCAT\"\namount = -1234\nmemo = \"zz memo\"\nline = transaction_row(txn_id, date, account, category, amount, memo)\nfields =
